# Rogue Trader Bestiary Reference
## Optimized for AI consumption — creature stat blocks for game implementation

---

## Stat Block Format

```
Name
WS  BS  S   T   Ag  Int Per WP  Fel
XX  XX  XX  XX  XX  XX  XX  XX  XX
Movement: Half/Full/Charge/Run    Wounds: XX
Armour: Description (Locations AP)    Total TB: X
Skills: ...
Talents: ...
Traits: ...
Weapons: Name (Damage Type; Pen X; Qualities)
```

### Key:
- (X) after S/T/Ag = Unnatural multiplier (e.g., S(10) with Unnatural Strength ×2 means SB=10)
- Movement: Half/Full/Charge/Run in metres
- TB = Toughness Bonus (including Unnatural multipliers)

---

### Clawed Fiend
| WS | BS | S | T | Ag | Int | Per | WP | Fel |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 43 | — | 59 | 53 | 47 | 16 | 54 | 23 | 04 |

- **Movement**: 5/10/15/30
- **Wounds**: 48
- **Armour**: Toughened Skin (All 4)
- **Total TB**: 10 (Unnatural Toughness ×2)
- **Skills**: Awareness (Per), Climb (S) +10
- **Talents**: Berserk Charge, Crushing Blow, Combat Master, Iron Jaw, Swift Attack, Takedown
- **Traits**: Bestial, Brutal Charge, Dark-Sight, Fear(2), Natural Armour, Improved Natural Weapons, Size(Hulking), Sturdy, Unnatural Strength(×2), Unnatural Toughness(×2), Unnatural Perception(×2)
- **Weapons**: Claws (1d10+10 R; Pen 1; Razor Sharp, Tearing), Tail Spike (1d10+2 R; Pen 0; Tearing)
- **Special — Bestial Fury**: If wounded in a previous round, gains Frenzy and Lightning Attack for remainder of combat

## Creature Traits Reference (Game-Relevant)

| Trait | Effect |
|---|---|
| Bestial | Cannot be reasoned with; immune to Fear, Pinning, Intimidation, social skills |
| Brutal Charge | +3 damage on Charge attacks |
| Daemonic(X) | Ignore first X damage per hit (like extra TB); immune to disease, poison |
| Dark-Sight | See in complete darkness |
| Fear(X) | Enemies must pass WP test or suffer penalties (1=normal, 2=-10, 3=-20, 4=-30) |
| Flyer(X) | Can fly; X = movement in metres |
| From Beyond | Immune to Fear, Pinning, Insanity, mind-affecting effects |
| Improved Natural Weapons | Natural weapons are no longer Primitive |
| Multiple Arms | Extra attacks or extra actions with additional limbs |
| Natural Armour(X) | AP X on all locations |
| Natural Weapons | Can make melee attacks dealing 1d10+SB damage |
| Regeneration(X) | Recovers X Wounds at start of each turn |
| Size(X) | Affects hit modifiers and wound threshold |
| Sturdy | Cannot be knocked down |
| Toxic | Target must pass Toughness test or take extra 1d10 damage (ignores armour) |
| Unnatural Characteristic(×X) | Characteristic Bonus is multiplied by X |
| Warp Instability | Takes damage when failing WP tests far from Warp |

### Size Categories
| Size | Modifier to Hit | Stealth Mod | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| Puny | -30 | +30 | Insect |
| Scrawny | -10 | +10 | Bird, small animal |
| Average | +0 | +0 | Human |
| Hulking | +10 | -10 | Ork, Ogryn |
| Enormous | +20 | -20 | Vehicle-sized |
| Massive | +30 | -30 | Titan, Void Kraken |

# Chapter IV: Liber Imperium Adversaries

> Non-vehicle creature and character stat blocks extracted from *The Liber Imperium* Chapter 13 (Adversaries). Vehicle and war-machine profiles live in `LiberImperium/vehicles.md`. Unnatural characteristics are shown here in the reference's `×N` form where the source gives a multiplier, or as a raw bonus in parentheses where only a bonus value is printed (e.g. `S 45 (6)` = Strength 45, Unnatural Strength bonus 6).

### Dark Disciple (Troop)
| WS | BS | S | T | Ag | Int | Per | WP | Fel |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 20 | 20 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 40 | 25 | 40 | 25 |

- **Movement**: 2/4/6/12
- **Wounds**: 6 | **Fatigue**: 6
- **Skills**: Deny The Witch +0, Forbidden Lore (Daemonology, Heresy, The Warp) +10, Invocation +10, Psyniscience, Scholastic Lore (Numerology, Occult) +10, Scrutiny +10
- **Talents**: Cold Hearted, Consumed By Spite, Jaded
- **Powers**: Has access to either the listed Summoning, Curses, or Rites, along with 4 of the Hexes listed:
  - *Summoning:* Summon the Malefic Horde, Summon the Lord of Skull's Soldiers, Summon the Dark Prince's Handmaidens, Summon the Cackling Spellfiends, Summon the Foetid Footmen, Summon the Wild Fury, Rite of Many Eyes
  - *Curses:* Curse of Choking Darkness, Curse of the Dark Mirror, Curse of Endless Terror, Curse of Madness, Curse of Misfortune
  - *Rites:* Rite of Arming, Rite of Infinite Passage, Rite of Sundering, Rite of Unholy Resurrection, Rite of Warding
  - *Hexes:* Hex of Conflagration -or- Hex of Contagion -or- Hex of Exsanguination, Hex of Impedent, Hex of Transfixation, Rune of Immunity, Rune of Metamorphosis, Rune of Opening, Rune of Summoning, Rune of Terror, Rune of Vigour, Vampiric Hex, Warp Tongue
- **Gear**: Ritual Tome (Summoning -or- Rites -or- Curses), Ritual Scrolls x4 (Any 4 Hexes), Ritual Kit (Summoning -or- Curses -or- Rites -or- Hexes), Book of Empty Promises -or- Malefic Codex
- **Armour**: Mech Robes (AP4, All)
- **Weapons**: Ritual Dagger

| Weapon | Class | Range | RoF | Damage | Pen | Qualities |
|--------|-------|-------|-----|--------|-----|-----------|
| Ritual Dagger | Melee | Melee | –/–/– | 1d5+7 R (2R+2 StB+3 CorrB) | 2 | Blood Drinker, Piercing, Tainted |

> **Consumed By Spite:** Those with the Talent receive a +10 bonus to resist Fear and any attempt to directly control or influence their minds. In addition, they receive a +30 bonus to resist the effects of Intimidation and Interrogation. They may also ignore the effects of being Stunned with a successful Difficult (–10) Discipline Test.

> **Of Infinite Forms:** A Chaos Spawn's form is very mutable. When combat begins, roll to determine its number of arms, Regeneration Trait, and Size Trait ranks. Once its Size Trait is determined, use the table below for quick reference.

**Chaos Spawn Size Modifiers**

| Size | Movement Rate | Wounds/Fatigue | Deadly Natural Weapons Damage/Pen | Reach |
|------|---------------|----------------|-----------------------------------|-------|
| 4 | 3/6/9/18   | 30/12 | 1d10+2 R +10 StB / Pen 2 | +0m |
| 5 | 4/8/12/24  | 40/22 | 1d10+2 R +10 StB / Pen 2 | +1m |
| 6 | 5/10/15/30 | 50/32 | 2d10+4 R +10 StB / Pen 4 | +1m |
| 7 | 6/12/18/36 | 60/42 | 2d10+4 R +10 StB / Pen 4 | +2m |
| 8 | 7/14/21/42 | 70/52 | 3d10+6 R +10 StB / Pen 6 | +2m |

> **Brief Candle:** A Chaos Spawn suffers levels of Fatigue equal to its Size Trait ranks at the end of each of its Turns. This continues once it is Unconscious upon reaching its Fatigue Threshold, and it dies upon reaching twice its Fatigue Threshold, as per usual.

- **Talents**: Adamantium Faith, Blazing Exemplar, Consumed By Spite, Eloquent, Air of Authority (3), Iron Discipline, Inspire Wrath, Jaded, Pure Faith, Radiant Presence, Righteous Oration, Shielding Faith, Unquestioning Devotion
- **Traits**: Touched by The Fates (4)
- **Powers**: Faith Powers (4 Fate Points): Choose one Aspect:

  - *The Emperor's Mercy:* Divine Endurance, Respite, Miraculous Recovery, No Rest for the Faithful, Resurrection, Righteous Frenzy
  - *The Emperor's Wrath:* Wrath of the Righteous, Hand of the Emperor, Divine Guidance, The Passion

> **Vile Manifestations:** The Master of Apostates has access to Faith Talents and Faith Powers, but these are not the true benificience of the God Emperor — they are the boon of some dark force, most likely the Dark Gods or their minions. The gameplay effects are as stated for the relevant Faith Power, but under the veneer of providence there is a dark taint nearly unmistakable to the initiated faithful or those with the Psyniscience Skill. He uses his Faith Powers as usual, spending Fate Points allotted by Touched By The Fates, but may also "Burn" (permanently lose) a Fate Point from that Trait to use the Burn cost of any of these Faith Powers.
>
> **Worthy of Greatness:** The Master of Apostates may have a Mark of Chaos rather than a Covenant of Chaos, if the GM finds it appropriate.

## IV.2 Chaos Space Marines (13.3.2)

All Chaos Space Marines share the **Astartes** trait bundle: Amphibious, Size (5), Space Marine Implants, Unnatural Strength (×4), Unnatural Toughness (×4), plus Fear (2) unless noted. Base Skills (most): Athletics +20, Awareness +10, Discipline +10, Dodge +10, Forbidden Lore (Heresy, The Warp) +0, Fortitude +20, Intimidate +10, Linguistics (Low Gothic, Unholy Tongue) +10, Parry +10. All Legion weapons carry the **Astartes** quality.

## IV.3 Daemons of Chaos (13.3.3)

> Non-vehicle daemon stat blocks extracted from *The Liber Imperium* §13.3.3. Unnatural characteristics are recorded as `base (bonus)` where the source prints a bonus value above the base (e.g. `40 (7)` = Strength 40, Unnatural Strength bonus 7). The corresponding Unnatural Trait is preserved in the Traits line with the source's rank in parentheses. Daemon-engine mounts (Chariots) use Wounds/Fatigue profiles rather than Hull Integrity and are included here.

- **Special — Talos Chainflail**: If the wielder fails a WS Test with it by more Degrees of Failure than his Weapon Skill Bonus, he resolves the attack as though he had struck himself.
- **Special — Talos Lightning Claw**: Deals +1 Damage per Degree of Success on the attack (+2 per Degree if used as a pair). Each claw is bulky and can only be used for gross motor skills.

### Deathmark (Elite)
| WS | BS | S | T | Ag | Int | Per | WP | Fel |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 30 | 50 | 45 (8) | 45 (8) | 35 | 40 | 45 | 40 | 15 |

- **Movement**: 4/8/-/-
- **Wounds**: 34 | **Fatigue**: 22
- **Skills**: Athletics +0, Awareness +20, Fortitude +10, Intimidate +0, Navigate (Surface) +20, Stealth +20
- **Talents**: Deadeye Shot, Deathdealer (Ranged), Heightened Senses (Sight), Inescapable Attack, Marksman, Overkill, Sharpshooter, Stealth Sniper, Vigilant
- **Traits**: Machine (8), Regeneration (4, Endless), Size (5), Unnatural Strength (×4), Unnatural Toughness (×4)
- **Armour**: Machine (8)
- **Weapons**: Synaptic Disintegrator (1d10+6 E; Pen 4; Heavy 150m; S/-/-; Accurate, Deadly, Living Ammunition, Shocking)
- **Special — Synaptic Disintegrator**: Only affects living beings. Psykers taking damage suffer -30 to all Focus Power Tests for one round. Any target taking Head-location damage must pass a Hard (-20) Fortitude Test or suffer 1d10 permanent Intelligence Damage.
- **Special — Marked for Death**: As a Half Action, the Deathmark marks one target within 50m (who may attempt a Hard (-20) Dodge Test to avoid it). The Deathmark suffers no penalties to hit a Marked target due to range, darkness, fog, or complete concealment (cover still provides AP). Aim time is halved (Half Action Aim gives +20, in-turn Free Action Aim gives +10). Only one Marked target at a time; the mark lasts about one hour unless dismissed.
- **Special — Dimensional Oubliette**: As a Half Action, the Deathmark opens a 2m×3m portal within 10m to a pocket dimension (closed with an in-turn Free Action). Creatures entering are subject to suffocation and vacuum and suffer -30 to sensory-perception tests.

- **Cybernetics**: Cyber-Construct Interface, Mind Impulse Unit

- **Special — Lores of the Crypteks**: A Cryptek may use one of several sets of "Psychic Powers" (a manipulation of physical reality, not true psychic power — cannot be resisted by Deny The Witch). Each use costs one Cognition Point; instead of a Focus Power Test the Cryptek makes a Tech-Use Test, counting its Psy Rating as equal to its Intelligence Bonus. Each Sustained Power imposes a cumulative -10 to further Tech-Use Tests. Choose one set: Chronomancer (Divination), Plasmancer (Pyromancy), Alchemist (Telekinesis), or Psychomancer (Telepathy).

- **Talents**: Battlefield Tech-Wright, Bulging Biceps, Furious Assault, Hardy, Iron Jaw, Street Fighting, Technical Knock, Unarmed Warrior, Weapon-Tech

- **Gear**: 2d10+20 Teef, Gubbins, Mek's Tools (as Combi-Tool), Oily Squig (as 1d5 Sacred Machine Oil), Grot Oiler (use the Gretchin profile with one Rank of Tech-Use; may Assist the Mekboy with Tech-Use Tests)
- **Armour**: Squig Leathers (AP2, Body, Arms, Legs) with Tool-Mount Modification (Mek's Tools), Flak Chestpiece (AP4, Body), Kustom Force Field (PR 50, OL 15)

- **Special — Mek Implants**: Function like Mechanicus Implants but are far cruder and more Ork-y in nature.
- **Special — Bodgin'**: Before battle a Mekboy can modify a number of Ork weapons up to his Intelligence Bonus (3), rolling on the Mek Kustom Job table for each weapon.
- **Special — Kustom Force Field**: Instead of the usual effects, when the Force Field Overloads it ceases to function for 1d5 Rounds and immediately inflicts 1d10+5 Energy Damage (Pen 4, Shocking) upon everything within 2d10 metres of the Mekboy, including himself.

- **Talents**: Furious Assault, Leap Up, Leaping Dodge, Leaping Strike, Lightning Reflexes, Resistance (Fear), Swift Attack
- **Traits**: Eaters of the Dead, Hyperactive Nyumen Organ, Natural Weapons (Beak), Unnatural Strength (×2), Unnatural Perception (×2)
- **Gear**: Cut meat of varying freshness, Fetish Pouch
- **Armour**: Hide Armour (AP2, All)

- **Special — Eaters of the Dead**: A Kroot who devours a fresh corpse (killed within 24 hours, organic — no daemons or machines) gains bonus Wounds equal to the corpse's unmodified Toughness Bonus (max the Kroot's own TB), lasting hours equal to the Kroot's TB or until lost. These bonus Wounds are removed by any successful attack's Damage before normal Wounds. While benefiting, the Kroot heals at twice the normal rate and adds one bonus Wound to any healing. Only one consumed corpse benefits at a time.
- **Special — Hyperactive Nyumen Organ**: The Kroot gains one additional Reaction per round. On a Full Move Action it may move an extra number of metres equal to its unmodified Agility Bonus; on a Run Action it may double its movement for one Round. Using this in two consecutive Turns inflicts one level of Fatigue unless a Challenging (+0) Fortitude Test is passed.

## IV.9 Tyranids (13.8.1–13.8.10)

> Non-vehicle Tyranid stat blocks extracted from *The Liber Imperium* §13.8. Unnatural characteristics are recorded as `base (bonus)` where the source prints a bonus multiplier/value beside the base (e.g. `35 (5)` where the source shows `(5)35`). The corresponding Unnatural Trait is preserved in the Traits line with the source's rank in parentheses. Psychic power lists (§13.8.3 Powers of the Hive Mind) are a spell list, not stat blocks, and are omitted. Winged Skyswarm organisms tagged "Aeronautica" that use Wounds profiles are included here as creatures; only Hull-Integrity vehicles are excluded.
>
> **Tyranid trait**: The creature is part of the Hive-Mind-ruled alien race; immune to cold, heat (except extremes), disease, and poison (including the Toxic Quality unless genophagic or Warp-based). May carry an Instinctive Behaviour (Feed/Lurk/Stalk) that governs its actions when out of Synapse range.
>
> **Synapse Creature trait**: The creature is a node of the Hive Mind. It grants immunity to Pinning and mind-affecting/Warp powers and the Fearless Talent to all other Tyranids within a range equal to its Willpower in metres, and suppresses Instinctive Behaviour in those creatures. A Tyranid Psyker always has both Synapse Creature and Shadow in the Warp.
>
> **Shadow in the Warp**: Non-Tyranid psykers within (creature's WP) metres suffer −20 to Focus Power Tests.

### 13.8.2 Tyranid Synapse Creatures

> Many Synapse Creatures "Choose two selections" from a shared bio-weapon armory. The damage of each melee bio-weapon scales with the wielder's Strength Bonus (StB) and Size/Creature Bonus (CB), so the printed damage differs per creature — the per-creature Weapons line below gives that creature's actual profiles where the source prints them.

#### Hive Tyrant (Master)
| WS | BS | S | T | Ag | Int | Per | WP | Fel |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 80 | 35 | 60 (14) | 50 (13) | 45 | 45 | 50 | 70 (11) | 00 |

- **Wounds**: 69 | **Fatigue**: 50
- **Skills**: Athletics +20, Awareness +10, Deny The Witch +20, Discipline +30, Dodge +0, Fortitude +20, Intimidate +30, Parry +10, Tactics (Assault Doctrine) +20
- **Talents**: Ambidextrous, Combat Master, Crushing Blow, Dual Strike, Fearless, Heightened Senses (All), Killing Strike, Lightning Attack, Never Die, Psy Rating (8), Swift Attack, Tear 'em Ter Bits!, Two Weapon Master (Melee)
- **Traits**: Brutal Charge (3), Dark-Sight, Deadly Natural Weapons (Bio-Weapons), Fear (4), From Beyond, Multiple Arms, Natural Armour (10), Psyker (Bound), Size (7), Touched By The Fates (3), Tyranid (Synapse Creature), Unnatural Strength (×8), Unnatural Toughness (×8), Unnatural Willpower (×4)
- **Powers**: Psyker (Bound, PR8). Biomancy: Mind Over Body (Master), Bio-Lightning, Hands of Steel, Flesh Like Iron, Healer, Seal Wounds, Regenerate, Endurance. Powers of the Hive Mind: Catalyst, Dominion, Hypnotic Gaze, Leech Essence, Onslaught, Paroxysm, Psychic Screech, The Horror, Warp Blast, Warp Field, Warp Lance
- **Gear**: —
- **Armour**: Natural Armour (10)
- **Weapons**: Choose two: Scything Talon (2d10+24 R [6R+14StB+4CB]; Pen 6; Melee+2m) -or- Rending Claw (2d10+22 R [4R+14StB+4CB]; Pen 9; Melee+2m; Razor Sharp) -or- Lash Whip (2d10+22 R [4R+14StB+4CB]; Pen 4; Melee+5m; Flexible, Reach, Snare (2)) & Bonesword (2d10+22 R [4R+14StB+4CB]; Pen 10; Melee+2m; Balanced, Deadly) -or- Twin Deathspitter (1d10+6 E; Pen 4; Heavy 100m; S/3/-; Blast (1), Corrosive (1d5), Living Ammunition, Twin-Linked) -or- Twin Devourer (1d10+5 R; Pen 0; Basic 30m; -/-/4; Living Ammunition, Storm, Twin-Linked) -or- Stranglethorn Cannon (3d10+6 I; Pen 4; 80m; S/-/-; Blast (4), Crippling (3), Living Ammunition, Snare (3)) -or- Heavy Venom Cannon (4d10+10 R; Pen 6; 100m; S/-/-; Blast (6), Corrosive (1d10), Living Ammunition, Toxic (4))
- **Special — Death Shock**: When the Hive Tyrant is slain, Tyranid creatures within synapse range (7m) without the Synapse Creature trait automatically break and flee (overriding Instinctive Behaviour). Any Synapse Creature within range must pass a Routine (+20) Discipline Test or be Stunned for 1d10 rounds.

- **Weapons**: 2 Sickle Talons (3d10+20 R [8R+12StB]; Pen 8; Melee+3m; Devastating (2), Tearing), 2 Rending Claws (3d10 R [6R+12StB]; Pen 11; Melee+3m; Razor Sharp), Thorax Spine-Maw (2d10+6 R; Pen 6; Melee+7m; Excruciating, Reach, Snare (4), Toxic (3))
- **Special — Movement/Fatigue**: Movement is printed as `12/32/72/72` (Run equals Charge) and Fatigue as `0`; both recorded verbatim from source (no correction invented).
- **Special — Thorax Spine-Maw**: A character caught by the Snare must make an Opposed Strength Test at the start of their turn; on failure they are dragged toward the Dimachaeron by metres equal to Degrees of Failure. Brought within 1m, they are instantly eaten.
- **Special — Terror Bio-Weapon**: Has Preferred Enemy (Chosen Target) and pursues it single-mindedly, ignoring the Instinctive Behaviour [Feed] trait until the target is devoured. It disengages other enemies to keep stalking, then attacks its prey with All Out Attacks using its Thorax Spine-Maw. Witnessing it devour its prey forces a Fear (3) Test on all who see it.

# Chapter V: Koronus Expanse Major Races (Core Rulebook)

> Core-rulebook Koronus Expanse NPC profiles (Imperial humans, servitors, xenos, and warp denizens) not duplicated elsewhere in this bestiary. A value in parentheses after a characteristic (e.g. `40 (8)`) is the value plus its Unnatural multiplier; the matching `Unnatural X (×N)` trait is listed in the Traits line.

## 5.1 Imperial Humans

### Colonist (base template) (Troop)
| WS | BS | S | T | Ag | Int | Per | WP | Fel |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 25 | 20 | 30 | 30 | 30 | 25 | 25 | 25 | 30 |

- **Movement**: 3/6/9/18
- **Wounds**: 9
- **Skills**: Awareness (Int), Common Lore (Imperium) (Int), Drive (pick one) (Ag), Speak Language (Low Gothic) (Int), Trade (pick one) (Int)
- **Talents**: Basic Weapon Training (SP), Melee Weapon Training (Primitive)
- **Gear**: Poor quality clothing, trinket, or votive item

The following are Minor NPC variants built on the Colonist template with the listed additions.

#### Adept
Use the Colonist profile modified as follows.
- **Characteristics**: Int 30
- **Skills**: Common Knowledge (Imperium) (Int) +10, Literacy (Int) +10, Speak Language (High Gothic) (Int)
- **Gear**: Quills, ink bottles, parchment, data-slate

Use the Colonist profile modified as follows.

- **Talents**: Basic Weapon Training (Primitive), Melee Weapon Training (Chain, Primitive)
- **Armour**: Leather and hide armour (All 2, Primitive)

- **Talents**: Jaded, Pistol Weapon Training (Universal)

- **Psychic Techniques**: Short Range Telepathy, Mind Probe, Terrify, Delude, Beastmaster, Compel
- **Armour**: Reinforced void suit (Arms 2, Body 3, Legs 2)
- **Weapons**: Sacrificial blade (1d5+4 R; Primitive), stub automatic (1d10+3 I; Pen 0; Pistol 30m; S/3/–; Clip 9; Reload Full)
- **Gear**: Tattered clothes, trinkets and fetishes, ragged reinforced void suit, respirator
- **Special — Dark Pact**: In addition to her psyker abilities, a Warp Witch can summon daemons and unclean spirits to help navigate the warp; these rituals are complex, dangerous, and cannot be performed during combat.

| WS | BS | S | T | Ag | Int | Per | WP | Fel |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 36 | — | 36 (8) | 40 | 45 (8) | 14 | 45 | 42 | — |

- **Talents**: Heightened Senses (all), Swift Attack, Lightning Attack
- **Traits**: Consume Life, Daemonic (TB 8), Dark-Sight, Daemonic Presence, Fear (2), Flyer (12), From Beyond, Hard Target, Phase, Toxic, Unnatural Agility (×2), Unnatural Speed (×2), Warp Instability
